<script id="search-result-tpl" type="text/x-template">
    <div class="media">
        <div class="media-left">
            <a href="#">
                <img class="media-object" src="{%=o.image%}" alt="{%=o.name%}">
            </a>
        </div>
        <div class="media-body">
            <h4 class="media-heading">{%=o.name%}</h4>
            {% if (o.price) { %}
                <div class="price-info">
                    {% if (o.price.type == 'gross_net')  { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.brutto%}</span>
                    {% } else if (o.price.type == 'net_gross') { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.netto%}</span>
                    {% } else if (o.price.type == 'only_net') { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.netto%}</span>
                    {% } else { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.brutto%}</span>
                    {% } %}
                </div>
            {% } %}
        </div>
    </div>
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(function($) {
        $('.search').submit(function() {
            var input = $(this).find('input[name=query]');

            if (!input.val()) {
                input.parent().addClass('has-error');
                return false;
            } 

            input.parent().removeClass('has-error');
            return true;            
        });

        var products = new Bloodhound({
            datumTokenizer: Bloodhound.tokenizers.obj.whitespace('name'),
            queryTokenizer: Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace,
            remote: {
                url: '/search/typeahead?query=%QUERY',
                wildcard: '%QUERY'
            },
            limit: 100
        });
     
        products.initialize();

        var tpl = tmpl($('#search-result-tpl').html());

        var typeahead = $('.search input[type="search"]');
        typeahead.typeahead({
            source: products.ttAdapter(),
            messages: {
                noresults: "Brak produktów dla wybranych kryteriów"
            }, 
            viewport: { selector: '#header-navbar', padding: 0 },   
            template: function(suggestions) {
                var content = '';
                for (var i = 0; i < suggestions.length; i++) {
                    content += '<li data-url="'+suggestions[i].url+'">'+tpl(suggestions[i])+'</li>';
                }
                return content;
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<div class="zrSearch">
<form action="/product/search" class="form-inline search" method="get">
<div class="zrClose"><img src="/images/frontend/theme/zara_2019/close.png"/></div>
<div class="text-center title">Co możemy dla Ciebie znaleźć w naszym magazynie</div>
<div class="input-group">
<label class="sr-only" for="search">Wyszukaj</label>
<input autocomplete="off" class="form-control bootstrap-typeahead" data-original-title="" name="query" placeholder="Wpisz czego szukasz" title="" type="search" value=""/>
<span class="input-group-btn">
<button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit"><img src="/images/frontend/theme/zara_2019/search.png"/></button>
</span>
</div>
</form>

<script id="search-result-tpl" type="text/x-template">
    <div class="media">
        <div class="media-left">
            <a href="#">
                <img class="media-object" src="{%=o.image%}" alt="{%=o.name%}">
            </a>
        </div>
        <div class="media-body">
            <h4 class="media-heading">{%=o.name%}</h4>
            {% if (o.price) { %}
                <div class="price-info">
                    {% if (o.price.type == 'gross_net')  { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.brutto%}</span>
                    {% } else if (o.price.type == 'net_gross') { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.netto%}</span>
                    {% } else if (o.price.type == 'only_net') { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.netto%}</span>
                    {% } else { %}
                        <span class="price">{%=o.price.brutto%}</span>
                    {% } %}
                </div>
            {% } %}
        </div>
    </div>
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(function($) {
        $('.search').submit(function() {
            var input = $(this).find('input[name=query]');

            if (!input.val()) {
                input.parent().addClass('has-error');
                return false;
            } 

            input.parent().removeClass('has-error');
            return true;            
        });

        var products = new Bloodhound({
            datumTokenizer: Bloodhound.tokenizers.obj.whitespace('name'),
            queryTokenizer: Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace,
            remote: {
                url: '/search/typeahead?query=%QUERY',
                wildcard: '%QUERY'
            },
            limit: 100
        });
     
        products.initialize();

        var tpl = tmpl($('#search-result-tpl').html());

        var typeahead = $('.search input[type="search"]');
        typeahead.typeahead({
            source: products.ttAdapter(),
            messages: {
                noresults: "Brak produktów dla wybranych kryteriów"
            }, 
            viewport: { selector: '#header-navbar', padding: 0 },   
            template: function(suggestions) {
                var content = '';
                for (var i = 0; i < suggestions.length; i++) {
                    content += '<li data-url="'+suggestions[i].url+'">'+tpl(suggestions[i])+'</li>';
                }
                return content;
            }
        });
    });
</script>

jQuery(function($) {
$(document).ready(function() {
	
	$('.btn-shopping-list-active').on('click', function(){
		if($(this).attr('type') == 'submit' && $(this).data('prezent')=='1' && $(this).data('hurt')=='0')
		{
			$.get('/gifts-show?product_id='+$(this).data('product')+'&option_id='+$(this).data('option'), function(result){
					$('#execute_time_overlay .modal-content').html(result);
					$('#execute_time_overlay').modal();
			});
			
			return false;
		}
	});
});

var autoplaySlider = function()
{
	var windows = $(window).width();
	
	if( windows > 1200)
	{		
		$('.presentation-products').slick({
	    dots: true,
	    infinite: true,
	    centerMode: true,
	    slidesToShow: 5,
	    slidesToScroll: 1,
		autoplay: true,
		autoplaySpeed: 3000,
		});
	}
	else if(windows > 767 && windows <= 1200)
	{
		$('.presentation-products').slick({
	    dots: true,
	    infinite: true,
	    centerMode: true,
	    slidesToShow: 3,
	    slidesToScroll: 1,
		autoplay: true,
		autoplaySpeed: 3000,
		});
	}	
	else{
		$('.presentation-products').slick({
	    dots: true,
	    infinite: true,
	    centerMode: true,
	    slidesToShow: 1,
	    slidesToScroll: 1,
		autoplay: true,
		autoplaySpeed: 3000,
		});
	}	
};

$(document).ready(function()
{
	autoplaySlider();
});

</script>

	function zr_zmien(id, input, opt)
	{
		var btn=document.getElementById(id);
		btn.setAttribute('type', 'submit');
		btn.setAttribute('data-option', opt);
		var inp = document.getElementById(input);
		inp.value = opt;
	}
	function zr_zmienView(id, input, opt, stock)
	{
		var btn=document.getElementById(id);
		btn.setAttribute('type', 'submit');
		btn.setAttribute('data-option', opt);
		var inp = document.getElementById(input);
		inp.value = opt;
		$('.red-info').html(stock);
	}
